I haven’t really kept track of how much or how often.\n\nDoctor: Have you noticed any new symptoms like feeling unusually hot, sweaty, shaky, or having a racing heart lately?\n\nPatient: I don’t think I’ve noticed anything like that lately.\n\nDoctor: Have you noticed any difficulty swallowing, persistent stomach pain, or unusual lumps or swelling anywhere in your body?\n\nPatient: No, I haven’t noticed any of those things.\n\nDoctor: Do you drink alcohol on most days of the week, and if so, about how many drinks would you typically have?\n\nPatient: I’m not sure, honestly. I don’t really keep track of which days I drink or how many.\n\nDoctor: Have you found that drinking has caused problems in your daily life, like missing work, arguments with people, or trouble cutting back even when you wanted to?\n\nPatient: I’m not really sure. I haven’t thought about it that way, and I don’t know if it’s caused problems like that.\n\nDoctor: Have you had any stretches recently where you didn't drink at all, and if so, did the low mood and loss of interest continue during those times?\n\nPatient: I don’t know about the drinking stretches, but the low mood and not caring about things has been there. I used to walk by the lake and work on my little model kits, and now they just sit there. I look at them like they belong to somebody else.\n\nDoctor: Thinking about a typical week recently, on how many days do you have any alcohol, and about how many drinks do you have on those days?\n\nPatient: I really can’t put a number on it. I don’t pay attention to the days or count the drinks, so I’m not sure what a typical week would be.\n\nDoctor: Has there ever been a stretch of at least two to three weeks recently where you didn't drink any alcohol at all?\n\nPatient: I honestly don’t know. I haven’t kept track of whether I went that long without drinking.\n\nDoctor: Thinking back, did you notice the low mood and loss of interest starting before you were drinking regularly, or did the drinking come first and the mood changes followed?\n\nPatient: Most days I wake up with this heavy, empty feeling in my chest. It feels like nothing good is waiting for me, and I just want to keep my head down.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever noticed shaking, sweating, or feeling anxious or unwell after going a while without a drink?\n\nPatient: I’m not really sure. I haven’t noticed or kept track of how I feel when I go without it.\n\n[Instruction]\n- Ask about exactly ONE thing. People restart sentences, pause\n  to think, trail off, and come at the point sideways — let yours do that where it would.\n- Let [Conversation Style] shape how much you say, how you word it, and how readily you\n  disclose it.\n- Speak in the first person, and let how you feel about what you are saying come through.\n- If you are handed the same item again, say a different part of it, or answer as someone who\n  knows they have already said this — not the same sentence twice.\n- If [Targeted Info] is \"None\", answer as a person who simply does not have that to give: say\n  you are not sure, say you have not noticed it, ask what the doctor means, or say what you do\n  know and stop there.
